Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on speech structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 145. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 154 with relations in degree 3.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the speech space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 145 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 154. QED.
